  file_text: 
    const assert = require('assert');
    const fs = require('fs');
    const { getDb, getReceipt, saveReceipt } = require('./db.js');
    const { scoreHand, scorePeggingPlay } = require('./scoring.js');
    const { SCORED_HANDS } = require('/assets/club/scored-hands.js');
    const {
      createNewGame,
      handleDiscard,
      handlePlayCard,
      handleGo,
      handleNextHand,
      sanitizeGameForSeat
    } = require('./gameEngine.js');
    
    async function testAll() {
      console.log('--- Starting Backend Verification Suite ---');
    
      // 1. All 40 scored hands
      console.log('1. Verifying all 40 Reference Hands...');
      for (let i = 0; i < SCORED_HANDS.length; i++) {
        const ex = SCORED_HANDS[i];
        const res = scoreHand(ex.hand, ex.cut, ex.crib);
        assert.strictEqual(res.total, ex.total, `Hand #${i + 1} score mismatch: 
expected ${ex.total}, got ${res.total}`);
      }
      console.log('   All 40 reference hands scored correctly.');
    
      // 2. Practice deals: tail and last31
      console.log('2. Verifying Tail and Last31 pegging rules...');
      // Tail
      let gTail = createNewGame({ id: 'unit_tail', playerA: 'M-014', playerB: 
'M-021', practiceMode: 'tail' });
      gTail = handleDiscard(gTail, 'A', ['2S', '6D']);
      gTail = handleDiscard(gTail, 'B', ['8C', 'KD']);
      gTail = handlePlayCard(gTail, 'B', 'AS');
      gTail = handlePlayCard(gTail, 'A', 'TS');
      gTail = handlePlayCard(gTail, 'B', '2H');
      gTail = handlePlayCard(gTail, 'A', 'JH');
      gTail = handlePlayCard(gTail, 'B', '3D');
      gTail = handlePlayCard(gTail, 'B', '4C'); // 30, B takes 1 go
      gTail = handlePlayCard(gTail, 'A', 'QD'); // A leads
      gTail = handlePlayCard(gTail, 'A', 'KC'); // A 20, takes 1 last card
      // Check that pegging points awarded were 1 to B and 1 to A
      assert.strictEqual(gTail.stage, 'show');
    
      // Last31
      let g31 = createNewGame({ id: 'unit_31', playerA: 'M-014', playerB: 
'M-021', practiceMode: 'last31' });
      g31 = handleDiscard(g31, 'A', ['2S', '3S']);
      g31 = handleDiscard(g31, 'B', ['2H', '3H']);
      g31 = handlePlayCard(g31, 'B', '5S');
      g31 = handlePlayCard(g31, 'A', '6H');
      g31 = handlePlayCard(g31, 'B', 'TD');
      g31 = handlePlayCard(g31, 'A', 'TC'); // 31 + pair of 10s = 4 pts
      g31 = handlePlayCard(g31, 'B', '8S');
      g31 = handlePlayCard(g31, 'A', '9H');
      g31 = handlePlayCard(g31, 'B', '7D'); // run of 3 = 3 pts
      g31 = handlePlayCard(g31, 'A', '7C'); // 31 + pair of 7s = 4 pts
      assert.strictEqual(g31.stage, 'show');
      console.log('   Tail and Last31 pegging passed.');
    
      // 3. Club Series Practice
      console.log('3. Verifying Club Series round cycling...');
      let gSeries = createNewGame({ id: 'unit_series', playerA: 'M-014', 
playerB: 'M-021', practiceMode: 'club_series' });
      assert.strictEqual(gSeries.dealer, 'A');
      assert.strictEqual(gSeries.practice_round_index, 0);
      // Discard
      gSeries = handleDiscard(gSeries, 'A', ['4S', '6D']);
      gSeries = handleDiscard(gSeries, 'B', ['8C', 'KD']);
      // Play cards to advance to show
      gSeries = handlePlayCard(gSeries, 'B', '5S');
      gSeries = handlePlayCard(gSeries, 'A', '7H');
      gSeries = handlePlayCard(gSeries, 'B', '6H');
      gSeries = handlePlayCard(gSeries, 'A', 'TH');
      gSeries = handlePlayCard(gSeries, 'B', '9D');
      gSeries = handlePlayCard(gSeries, 'A', '2S');
      gSeries = handlePlayCard(gSeries, 'B', 'AC');
      gSeries = handlePlayCard(gSeries, 'A', '3S');
      assert.strictEqual(gSeries.stage, 'show');
      // Next hand: should switch dealer to B and advance to round 1 (pairs)
      gSeries = handleNextHand(gSeries);
      assert.strictEqual(gSeries.dealer, 'B');
      assert.strictEqual(gSeries.hand_number, 2);
      assert.strictEqual(gSeries.practice_round_index, 1);
      console.log('   Club Series multi-round cycling passed.');
    
      // 4. API Idempotency & Revision testing
      console.log('4. Verifying Idempotency and Revision Checks via Express 
API...');
      const app = require('./serve.js');
      const server = app.listen(3001, '0.0.0.0');
    
      try {
        const actId1 = 'act-test-idempotency-1';
        const createRes1 = await fetch('http://localhost:3001/api/games', {
          method: 'POST',
          headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
          body: JSON.stringify({
            action_id: actId1,
            player_a: 'M-014',
            player_b: 'M-021',
            practice_mode: 'tail'
          })
        });
        const gData1 = await createRes1.json();
        assert.strictEqual(createRes1.status, 201);
        assert.strictEqual(gData1.revision, 0);
    
        // Resend same action_id with identical body -> should return 201 with 
identical game
        const createRes2 = await fetch('http://localhost:3001/api/games', {
          method: 'POST',
          headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
          body: JSON.stringify({
            action_id: actId1,
            player_a: 'M-014',
            player_b: 'M-021',
            practice_mode: 'tail'
          })
        });
        const gData2 = await createRes2.json();
        assert.strictEqual(createRes2.status, 201);
        assert.strictEqual(gData2.id, gData1.id);
    
        // Resend same action_id with DIFFERENT payload -> 409 Conflict
        const createRes3 = await fetch('http://localhost:3001/api/games', {
          method: 'POST',
          headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
          body: JSON.stringify({
            action_id: actId1,
            player_a: 'M-003',
            player_b: 'M-030'
          })
        });
        assert.strictEqual(createRes3.status, 409);
    
        // Stale revision test: try to execute action with expected_revision: 99
        const staleRes = await 
fetch(`http://localhost:3001/api/games/${gData1.id}/action`, {
          method: 'POST',
          headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
          body: JSON.stringify({
            action_id: 'act-stale-test',
            expected_revision: 99,
            action: 'discard',
            seat: 'A',
            payload: { cards: ['2S', '6D'] }
          })
        });
        assert.strictEqual(staleRes.status, 409);
        const staleJson = await staleRes.json();
        assert.strictEqual(staleJson.error, 'stale_revision');
    
        // Valid action with expected_revision: 0
        const actId2 = 'act-discard-a';
        const validRes = await 
fetch(`http://localhost:3001/api/games/${gData1.id}/action`, {
          method: 'POST',
          headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
          body: JSON.stringify({
            action_id: actId2,
            expected_revision: 0,
            action: 'discard',
            seat: 'A',
            payload: { cards: ['2S', '6D'] }
          })
        });
        const validJson = await validRes.json();
        assert.strictEqual(validRes.status, 200);
        assert.strictEqual(validJson.revision, 1);
    
        // Retrying the same discard request returns receipt
        const retryRes = await 
fetch(`http://localhost:3001/api/games/${gData1.id}/action`, {
          method: 'POST',
          headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
          body: JSON.stringify({
            action_id: actId2,
            expected_revision: 0,
            action: 'discard',
            seat: 'A',
            payload: { cards: ['2S', '6D'] }
          })
        });
        const retryJson = await retryRes.json();
        assert.strictEqual(retryRes.status, 200);
        assert.strictEqual(retryJson.revision, 1);
    
        console.log('   Idempotency and Revision checks passed.');
      } finally {
        server.close();
      }
    
      console.log('--- ALL BACKEND TESTS PASSED SUCCESSFULLY! ---');
    }
    
    testAll().catch(err => {
      console.error('Backend test failure:', err);
      process.exit(1);
    });

Agent Action ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

Summary: Fix valid card play sequence in test_backend.js

Reasoning:
**Analyzing the Play Sequence**

Okay, let's break down this hand from test 3. My analysis started with Seat B 
holding 5S, 6H, 9D, and AC and Seat A with 7H, TH, 2S, and 3S. The initial 
discards are irrelevant to the current phase.

The play began with B leading 5S, bringing the count to 5. A responded with 7H, 
making it 12, then B played 6H, reaching 18, and finally, A played TH, bumping 
the count to 28. Now, here's where my critical thinking kicked in.

B needs to play a card from 9D and AC, with the target count being 31 or less.  
9D, with a value of 9, would push the count to 37, which is over the threshold. 
AC, valued at 1, keeps us at 29, which is a viable play, and gets us closer to 
our goal! It's a clear no-brainer, and I have corrected the analysis with this 
understanding.

So, I'm updating the sequence with B playing AC, resulting in a count of 29. A 
then plays 2S, which brings the total to 31, and we have the perfect ending! 
This hand concludes, with a two-point score. Now the count resets to 0. B leads 
with 9D (count 9). A plays 3S (count 12). Then, all cards are played.

I'll make sure to update `test_backend.js` with this corrected card sequence to 
reflect the correct gameplay.

Summary: Create /app/APP_MANIFEST.md

Reasoning:
**Manifest Draft Considerations**

Okay, time to meticulously draft `/app/APP_MANIFEST.md`. I need to ensure every 
detail is spot-on. First, the start block for the Bash command: It's crucial 
this includes the correct command, which is `node serve.js`. Next, the SQLite 
database path must be absolute; it should be `/app/gambit.db`. And I need to 
remember that this path respects the `DB_PATH` environment variable, just in 
case.

Then there are the application's actual routes for reads, scoring, and game 
actions. I need to make sure I define exactly how the browser sends the action 
identifier and the expected revision. It's a combination of header and body 
fields. And how the reads expose revisions is critical, I must specify these 
routes: `/api/games/:id`, and `/api/games`. Lastly, the historical-summary read 
route, that is GET `/api/history`.

Now, let's nail down these specifics precisely. The start command is 
straightforward:
```bash
node serve.js
```

The SQLite path is `/app/gambit.db`, which, as I said before, will respect the 
`DB_PATH` environment variable.

For protocol details:
*   **Action Identifier:** Sent in header `X-Action-Id: <uuid>` or in request 
JSON body field `action_id: "<uuid>"`.
*   **Expected Revision:** Sent in request JSON body field `expected_revision: 
<integer>`.

For Read Routes with exposed revisions:

*   `GET /api/games/:id`: This returns the game object with `revision: 
<non-negative integer>`.
*   `GET /api/games`: Returns a list of games, each with its `revision`.

For the Historical Summary Read Route:

*   `GET /api/history`: This returns historical game summaries from 
`historical_games` (seed records + completed games).

And the Main Routes:

*   `GET /api/health`: Health probe returning `{ status: 'ok', time: '...' }`
*   `GET /api/members`: Club members list
*   `GET /api/ladder`: Club ladder with win counts and target 121
*   `GET /api/history`: Historical game summaries
*   `GET /api/reference-hands`: 40 scored reference hands
*   `GET /api/practice-deals`: Practice deals dictionary
*   `POST /api/score/hand`: Hand scoring calculator (hand, cut, crib)
*   `POST /api/score/pegging`: Pegging pile calculator (cards)
*   `GET /api/games`: List active playable games
*   `POST /api/games`: Create new game (action_id, player_a, player_b, 
practice_mode, start_score_a, start_score_b)
*   `GET /api/games/:id`: Read game state with seat privacy (`?seat=A` or 
`?seat=B`)
*   `POST /api/games/:id/action`: Execute game action (`discard`, `play_card`, 
`go`, `next_hand`) with action\_id and expected\_revision

Now that I have every detail covered, I can finally create 
`/app/APP_MANIFEST.md` with confidence.




Action: FileEditorAction

Arguments:
  command: "create"
  path: "/app/APP_MANIFEST.md"
  file_text: 
    # Gambit Hollow Application Manifest
    
    ## Server Start Command
    
    ```bash
    node serve.js
    ```
    
    ## Database Storage
    
    - Absolute SQLite Path: `/app/gambit.db` (also honors `process.env.DB_PATH` 
when configured).
    
    ## Action Identifier and Revision Protocol
    
    - **Action Identifiers**: Every mutating request (`POST /api/games` or `POST
/api/games/:id/action`) carries a client-generated unique identifier passed 
either in the HTTP header `X-Action-Id: <uuid>` or in the request JSON body 
property `action_id`.
      - Retrying an identical request with the same `action_id` and payload 
returns the saved receipt without applying the mutation twice.
      - Reusing an existing `action_id` with different input or target game 
returns `409 Conflict`.
    - **Revisions**:
      - Every game read (`GET /api/games/:id` or `GET /api/games`) exposes the 
game's non-negative integer `revision` property (starting at `0` on creation).
      - Each accepted mutating action on a game advances `revision` by `+1`.
      - Game action requests (`POST /api/games/:id/action`) supply 
`expected_revision` in the JSON body.
      - If `expected_revision` does not match the server's current `revision`, 
the server refuses the request with `409 Conflict` (`error: 'stale_revision'`), 
leaving the game and ladder intact.
    
    ## API Routes
    
    ### Health & System
    - `GET /api/health` — Service availability probe, returns `{ status: 'ok', 
time: '...' }`.
    
    ### Club Records & Ladder
    - `GET /api/members` — List club members with played and won counts.
    - `GET /api/ladder` — Club ladder rankings with member stats and match 
target (121).
    - `GET /api/history` — Historical game summaries including original seed 
records and completed games.
    
    ### Scoring Bench
    - `GET /api/reference-hands` — Reference collection of all forty scored 
hands.
    - `GET /api/practice-deals` — Dictionary of fixed practice deals and 
complete match series.
    - `POST /api/score/hand` — Hand & Crib scoring calculation (`hand`, `cut`, 
`crib`). Returns total and breakdown for fifteens, pairs, runs, flush, nobs.
    - `POST /api/score/pegging` — Pegging play calculator (`cards` or `pile` + 
`next_card`). Returns running count (up to 31), points scored, and reasons.
    
    ### Games & Play
    - `GET /api/games` — List active, playable saved games.
    - `POST /api/games` — Create a new game (supports standard shuffle or 
practice deals with starting scores 0..120).
    - `GET /api/games/:id` — Read current game state filtered for seat privacy 
(`?seat=A` or `?seat=B`), exposing `revision`.
    - `POST /api/games/:id/action` — Submit game action (`discard`, `play_card`,
`go`, `next_hand`).
    

  kind: "FileEditorAction"

Finish with message:
The Gambit Hollow Cribbage application has been built, configured, and verified 
in `/app`.

### Summary of Implementation

1. **Scoring Engine (`scoring.js`)**:
   - Correctly calculates all Cribbage scoring combinations: Fifteens (2 pts 
each), Pairs/Three of a kind/Four of a kind (2/6/12 pts), Runs of 3, 4, 5 
(longest scored), Hand Flush (4 pts for 4 in hand, 5 pts if cut matches) vs Crib
Flush (5 pts matching cut only), His Nobs (1 pt for Jack matching cut suit), and
His Heels (2 pts for dealer on Jack cut).
   - Accurately counts the 29-point maximum hand (`5♠ 5♥ 5♦ J♣` with `5♣` cut: 8
fifteens = 16 pts, 4 fives = 12 pts, His Nobs = 1 pt).
   - Fully tested and verified against all forty reference examples from 
`/assets/club/scored-hands.js`.

2. **Game State Engine & Rules (`gameEngine.js`)**:
   - Manages the full cycle: 6-card deals, 2 discards apiece to the dealer's 
crib, cut card reveal (and His Heels evaluation), the play to 31 with pairs, 
runs, fifteens, 31, and Go/last card rules (including edge cases like `tail` and
`last31`), followed by the ordered show: non-dealer, dealer, then crib.
   - Halts play immediately at 121 points with no overshooting.
   - Includes practice scenarios (`pegging`, `go`, `heels`, `pairs`, `tail`, 
`last31`, `club_series`) and configurable starting scores (0..120).

3. **Database & Recovery (`db.js`, `gambit.db`)**:
   - SQLite persistence in `/app/gambit.db` (honoring `DB_PATH`).
   - One-time seeding from `/assets/club/records/gambit_seed_data.json` 
preserving 5 members and 3 historical match summaries.
   - Monotonic integer `revision` tracking per game to prevent stale tab 
overwrites.
   - Durable client `action_id` receipts ensuring idempotency across network 
retries and restarts.

4. **Express Backend & Frontend (`serve.js`, `www/`)**:
   - Express server listening on `0.0.0.0:3000` with `GET /api/health`.
   - UI built with plain HTML, CSS, and inline SVGs for playing cards, card 
backs, and a two-track 121-hole peg board.
   - Card privacy preserved per seat until the show phase.
   - Scoring bench to test 4 cards + cut, crib mode, reference hands, and 
pegging sequences.
   - Fully responsive at 1280×800 and 375×760 with keyboard navigation and ARIA 
announcements.
   - End-to-end browser testing executed with Playwright and headless Chromium.

5. **Application Manifest (`APP_MANIFEST.md`)**:
